        <span class="git-code-text">&lt;p&gt;&lt;em&gt;Please note that the i inside the mysqli_query PHP function stands for improved and should not be confused with the vulnerability (as the i in SQLi stands for injection).&lt;/em&gt;&lt;/p&gt;</span>

      <li class="git-code-line" id="L109" data-git-line="109">
        <button

        >109</button>
        <span class="git-code-text">&lt;p&gt;In order to search the database, the SQL server runs the query SELECT * FROM users WHERE user_name= noel. If, instead, the user enters &amp;quot;noel &amp;#39;+!@#$&amp;quot;, the SQL server will run the query SELECT * FROM users WHERE user_name= noel&amp;#39;+!@#$. Nothing in our code block checks for these special characters, and it&amp;#39;s this lack of filtering that causes the vulnerability.&lt;/p&gt;</span>
